13699 FDAE C03A  30 	mov *r10+,r0
13700 FDB0 0450  20 	b *r0
13701               	;
13702               	; 	' EVERYTHING BELOW THIS LINE IS ASSEMBLED INTO BANK 1, so the INCLUDE order
13703               	; 	' is load-bearing and nothing but data may follow it.
13704               	; 	'
13705               	; 	' THE FONT IS NOW IN THE BANK TOO, and it used to be deliberately outside:
13706               	; 	' keeping one readable thing in bank 0 means a bank-selection mistake shows
13707               	; 	' as "text survives, art does not" rather than a uniformly blank screen,
13708               	; 	' which is a genuinely useful thing to have when a bank goes wrong.
13709               	; 	'
13710               	; 	' It was given up for 472 bytes, because the fixed area had 342 left and
13711               	; 	' that diagnostic is worth less than the ability to keep building. Two
13712               	; 	' things make it a fair trade rather than a straight loss:
13713               	; 	'
13714               	; 	'   * `BANK SELECT 1` already runs BEFORE `DEFINE CHAR 32,59,font_bits`
13715               	; 	'     (see setup), so nothing about the read order had to change. If that
13716               	; 	'     ever stops being true the font goes blank at boot, immediately and
13717               	; 	'     unmistakably -- a loud failure, not a subtle one.
13718               	; 	'   * The alternative on the table was banking the 29 PRINT AT literals,
13719               	; 	'     which recovers LESS (about 360 bytes net after the reader routine)
13720               	; 	'     and would blind assets/checklayout.py, whose whole method is parsing
13721               	; 	'     `PRINT AT n,"literal"` to catch a string running past column 31 or
13722               	; 	'     a HUD poke landing inside a label. Trading a diagnostic for bytes is
13723               	; 	'     one thing; trading a build gate for fewer bytes is another.
13724               	; 	#if TI994A
13725               	; 	BANK 1
13726      024C     BANK_0_FREE:	EQU >fffe-$
     **** ****     > .rept

13864               	;
13865               	; 	' THE COLOUR TABLE, 472 bytes of the same value ($14 = black ink on dark blue).
13866               	; 	'
13867               	; 	' Loaded with DEFINE COLOR 32,59,font_col, which is ONE synchronous call that
13868               	; 	' writes all three screen thirds (define_color always does the LDIRVM3 triple
13869               	; 	' copy). It replaced a VPOKE loop that wrote this one value 1,416 times paced by
13870               	; 	' 24 WAITs -- the largest scripted delay in the boot, and the reason the title
13871               	; 	' screen used to fill in visibly rather than appearing at once.
13872               	; 	'
13873               	; 	' EIGHT BYTES PER CHARACTER, not one. Supply fewer and DEFINE COLOR reads
13874               	; 	' whatever follows in ROM as colour data, with no error at build or run time.
13875               	;
13876               	; font_col:
13877               cvb_FONT_COL

13996               	; 	INCLUDE "art.bas"
13997               	; 	' Keystone Kapers art -- GENERATED by assets/genart.py. Do not edit; regenerate.
13998               	; 	'
13999               	; 	' EVERY SPRITE IS 16x16: sprite size and magnification are GLOBAL on the
14000               	; 	' TMS9918, so small objects are drawn small INSIDE a 16x16 box rather than
14001               	; 	' being small sprites.
14002               	; 	'
14003               	; 	' EVERY 8-PX OBJECT SITS IN ROWS 8-15 OF ITS BOX, ducked Kelly included, so
14004               	; 	' one rule places them all: bottom edge = y + 16, and a thing resting on the
14005               	; 	' floor is at y = FLOORY - 16.
14006               	; 	'
14007               	; 	' Sprite bytes are QUADRANT-ORDERED: 16 rows of the left half, then 16 of
14008               	; 	' the right. Every block is an EVEN number of bytes -- an odd DATA BYTE run
14009               	; 	' silently misaligns every word table after it (CLAUDE.md 3A).
14010               	;
14011               	; spr_kelly:	' Kelly: RIGHT hat/face/tunic/tunic-arm-up/duck-hat/duck-face, then the same LEFT (+24), then the FOUR shared run frames. Patterns 0..60
14012               cvb_SPR_KELLY
14013               	; 	DATA BYTE $00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00
14014 63DA 0000     	byte >00,>00,>00,>00,>00,>00,>00,>00
     63DC 0000     
     63DE 0000     
     63E0 0000

15008               	;
15009               	; 	' -------------------------------------------------- scanner canvas
15010               	; 	' Chars 208-255: 16 cols x 3 rows over screen rows 21-23. Blank to start;
15011               	; 	' dots are plotted by VPOKEing the PATTERN table (rows 21-23 sit in the
15012               	; 	' third screen third, so every scanner pattern is at base 4096).
15013               	;
15014               	; 	' --------------------------------------------------- font colours
15015               	; 	' Chars 32-90, EIGHT bytes each. Without this the font keeps whatever
15016               	; 	' CVBasic left in the colour table, which on a green store made the HUD
15017               	; 	' unreadable.
15018               	;
15019               	; scan_col3:	' one 8-byte block per canvas row: 4 px of GREY margin, then a 3 px band of furniture GREY and a yellow floor line, per 4 px level, on dark green. The Kop and the crook are coloured PER CHARACTER at run time -- see scan_set
15020               cvb_SCAN_COL3
15021               	; 	DATA BYTE $EE,$EE,$EE,$EE,$EC,$EC,$EC,$BC
15022 728A EEEE     	byte >ee,>ee,>ee,>ee,>ec,>ec,>ec,>bc
     728C EEEE     
     728E ECEC     
     7290 ECBC     
15023               	; 	DATA BYTE $EC,$EC,$EC,$BC,$EC,$EC,$EC,$BC

     7294 ECBC     
     7296 ECEC     
     7298 ECBC

15026 729A ECEC     	byte >ec,>ec,>ec,>bc,>ee,>ee,>ee,>ee
     729C ECBC     
     729E EEEE     
     72A0 EEEE

15131               	; 	INCLUDE "store.bas"
15132               	; 	' Keystone Kapers store -- GENERATED by assets/genstore.py. Do not edit.
15133               	; 	'
15134               	; 	' EIGHT band templates of 5 rows x 32 cols (160 bytes each) plus a 32-byte
15135               	; 	' index over (level, screen). The whole 8-screen, 4-level building is about
15136               	; 	' 1.3 KB instead of the 5,120 bytes it would cost to store literally.
15137               	; 	'
15138               	; 	' All eight templates are ONE contiguous block under ONE label, so a band
15139               	; 	' blit is
15140               	; 	'     SCREEN stor_tpl, tpl*160, toprow*32, 32, 5, 32
15141               	; 	' with no label ladder -- SCREEN needs a literal label and a label cannot be
15142               	; 	' chosen by a variable, which is why RallyX needed a 4-way IF over its maze
15143               	; 	' tables. One block sidesteps it.
15144               	; 	'
15145               	; 	' 160 is EVEN, so this cannot leave the location counter odd and misalign
15146               	; 	' the word tables defined after it.
15147               	; 	'
15148               	; 	' template ids:
15149               	; 	'   0 T_AISLE_A
15150               	; 	'   1 T_AISLE_B
15151               	; 	'   2 T_ESC_W
15152               	; 	'   3 T_ESC_E
15153               	; 	'   4 T_ELEV
15154               	; 	'   5 T_ROOF0
15155               	; 	'   6 T_ROOF1
15156               	; 	'   7 T_ROOF2
15157               	; 	'   8 T_ROOF3
15158               	; 	'   9 T_ROOF4
15159               	; 	'   10 T_ROOF5
15160               	; 	'   11 T_ROOF6
15161               	; 	'   12 T_ROOF7
15162               	; 	'   13 T_END_W
15163               	; 	'   14 T_END_E
15164               	;
15165               	; stor_tpl:	' 15 templates x 160 bytes
15166               cvb_STOR_TPL
15167               	; 	DATA BYTE 154,154,154,154,107,154,154,154,154,154,154,107,154,154,154,154
